### 2026-09-10 14:05:34  #MKT News #Important

【DeepSeek launches V4.1 Flash model】Sept 10 — DeepSeek launched its V4.1 Flash model, the smallest in its new architecture series, with native multimodal visual understanding. The company said the model sharply reduces KV cache size: HBM requirements drop to one-quarter and SSD requirements to one-eighth versus the prior generation. DeepSeek added that KV cache compression materially lowers operating costs for agent-style workloads, where cache-hit costs are a significant share of expenses.

### 2026-09-10 14:03:22  #MKT News #Important #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #Market Regions #Europe & UK #EURUSD #Impact mixed #DXY #XAUUSD #Impact bearish

【ECB preview: 25bp hike all but certain; subsequent path is main market focus】Major banks and a Reuters survey largely expect the ECB to deliver a 25bp hike at the upcoming meeting; markets will be watching forward guidance and odds of further tightening more than the decision itself. Goldman Sachs: base case 25bp and end of this tightening cycle after September, but risk of a December hike rises if energy prices stay high, inflation re-accelerates or the Fed tightens further. ING: expects a 25bp hike but characterizes it as dovish — post-hike signals likely insufficient to justify market-priced further tightening. Société Générale: 25bp with a relatively hawkish stance to counter energy-driven inflation. Reuters survey: 25bp then a stop, with the deposit rate staying at 2.50% through end‑2026. Danske Bank: 25bp; Lagarde will keep flexibility without committing to a path and is unlikely to explicitly try to suppress tightening expectations. Deutsche Bank: 25bp and a further hike in December is likely; 2.75% is a more probable terminal rate unless geopolitics ease and growth weakens, which could cap rates at 2.5%. Natixis: 25bp, followed by neutral messaging to avoid implying a long hike cycle; expects a pause until end‑2027. RABOBANK: 25bp with more restrained guidance and no push toward a ~3% terminal rate. SEB: 25bp and likely the last hike of the cycle, though uncertainty remains; persistently above‑target inflation is unlikely, supporting a prolonged pause. BNP Paribas SA: sees potential hikes in September and December if growth and inflation forecasts are revised up at the September meeting. MUFG: 25bp fully priced; market reaction will hinge on forward guidance — lack of clear support for a year‑end hike would likely weaken the euro. State Street Global: ECB may pair a 25bp increase with intentionally open signals; key questions are whether 2.50% is judged sufficiently restrictive and whether the bank preserves policy space for December.

### 2026-09-10 14:02:53  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #US10Y #Impact bullish #DXY #S&P500 #Impact mixed

【Institutions' forecasts and market bets diverge; Sep 25bp hike shifts from tail risk to main trade】Prediction market Polymarket prices a Sep 25bp Fed hike at 54%; rate derivatives imply 63.6% — roughly a 10pp gap but same direction. Resilient employment, rising oil-driven inf risk and a hawkish Fed tilt have moved a Sep hike from tail risk to the primary market scenario. More than 50% odds do not guarantee a hike: markets still assign 36–47% probability of no change and any Fed decision could trigger re-pricing. Friday's inf release will be decisive; a REUTERS SURVEY shows 70% of economists expect no hike, underlining the lack of consensus between institutional forecasts and trading markets.

### 2026-09-10 14:00:18  #其他

【8月我國新能源汽車產銷量同比分別增長18.9%和17.8%】中國汽車工業協會今天公佈了8月份的汽車產銷資料，我國新能源汽車主導地位持續鞏固，成為穩定行業大盤的關鍵增量。最新資料顯示，新能源汽車月度銷量佔比再創新高，8月份，新能源汽車產銷量分別完成165.3萬輛和164.3萬輛，同比分別增長18.9%和17.8%，新能源汽車新車銷量達到汽車新車總銷量的60.6%。在出口方面，8月份，我國汽車出口101萬輛，同比增長65.3%，月度出口量連續3個月超過100萬輛。其中，新能源汽車出口52.6萬輛，同比增長1.3倍。在出口結構方面，新能源汽車出口量佔全部汽車出口量比重已連續三個月超過50%。中國汽車工業協會副秘書長陳士華介紹，新能源汽車出口佔比連續三個月超過50%，意味著每出口兩輛車，就有一輛是新能源汽車。這種出口結構的根本性變化，說明中國汽車出口正在向“新能源汽車引領”切換，正在靠電動化技術路線開啟全球市場。

【Lagarde's inflation emphasis could signal ECB rate path】Royal London Asset Management analyst Wolfgang Baur said ahead of Thursday's ECB meeting markets largely expect a 25bp hike after June's first move, and the key question is whether that marks the end of a short-lived pre-emptive tightening or the start of a longer hiking cycle. Market clues will come from whether Lagarde stresses energy-driven headline inflation or recent weakness in core and services inflation. Rising long-term government bond yields have already tightened financial conditions and are carrying much of the ECB's work. Baur said he finds it hard to justify a longer, more aggressive hiking cycle unless inflationary pressures broaden materially.

### 2026-09-10 13:45:58  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Palm Oil Under Pressure After Bearish MPOB Data — Malaysian palm oil futures fell around 1.3% to MYR 4,900 per tonne, extending their recent drop to a one-week low. Sentiment was pressured after Malaysian Palm Oil Board data showed inventories rose 7.48% mom in August to an eight-month high of 2.82 million tonnes. Meanwhile, exports fell 7.5% to 1.29 million tonnes, with production adding 1.39% to 1.82 million tonnes, reinforcing concerns over ample supply. Risk appetite was further weighed by weaker edible oil prices in Dalian and Chicago and a stronger ringgit. In India, heavy edible oil buying has congested major ports, delaying vessel unloading by up to 10 days as storage tanks overflow, potentially curbing near-term import demand. Still, losses were capped by elevated crude oil prices, with Brent breaching US$100 a barrel amid renewed Middle East tensions, boosting palm oil’s appeal as a biodiesel feedstock. Dry weather across Southeast Asia also fueled concerns over yields following fires and haze in Borneo and Sumatra.

【上海體育局回應劉翔安置工作，未來將持續最佳化退役運動員安置】劉翔同志是我國優秀田徑運動員，也是鐫刻上海歷史的功勳運動員，為體育事業作出了突出貢獻。前期，經與劉翔本人深入溝通，提供了包括田徑中心負責人、技術總監、體育類高校等組織安置方向，劉翔最終選擇自主擇業，我們予以充分尊重。9月1日，劉翔遞交了自願選擇自主擇業申請表，並簽署了有關協議；9月7日，人事關係手續基本辦理完結，並按規定給予了自主擇業經濟補償金。對於劉翔同志將2015年4月以來全部工資收入捐贈用於西藏吉隆泥石流救災的善舉，我們深表敬意！本市運動員安置工作依據國家及本市事業單位人事管理、退役運動員安置相關政策實施。組織安置、自主擇業是政策規定的兩條安置途徑，過去十年間，本市對近千名運動員進行了退役安置，其中包括10餘位獲得奧運冠軍、世界冠軍的運動員。我們會繼續關心好包括劉翔在內的所有退役運動員，也祝願他們在人生新賽道上續寫精彩！我們將認真吸納各方面意見建議，持續最佳化本市退役運動員安置政策，健全運動員轉型發展支援體系，切實維護好退役運動員合法權益。衷心感謝社會各界對體育事業和退役運動員安置工作的關心、監督。

NZX 50 Ends 0.8% Lower, Nears 6-Week Low — New Zealand stocks dropped 108 points, or 0.8%, to close at 13,711 on Thursday, touching its lowest level since July 31, after rising in the previous session, tracking a downbeat session on Wall Street overnight following a surge in Treasury yields and rising oil prices. Oil continued to rise, raising concerns over inflation and boosting bets that central banks would raise interest rates, including the RBNZ, which recently hiked its interest rate for the second straight meeting. Traders also anticipated the release of US inflation data later this week for clues on the Fed's monetary policy decision at its upcoming meeting, as well as New Zealand's manufacturing PMI for August due on Friday. Industrials, utilities, and financials mainly weighed on the index, with the biggest laggards including Fletcher Building (-2.6%), Delegat Group (-2.1%), Briscoe Group (-1.9%), ANZ Group (-1.5%), Mainfreight (-1.3%), Westpac Banking Corp. (-1.2%), and Freightways Group (-1.2%).

### 2026-09-10 13:19:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #Finland #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Finnish Industrial Output Growth Hits 4-Month High — Industrial production in Finland rose by 4.5% year-on-year in July 2026, marking the strongest growth since March and accelerating from a downwardly revised 4.4% gain in the previous month. The increase was largely driven by higher output in the manufacturing sector, rising 5.3% after a 4.1% gain in June, as increased activity in the metal (9.5% vs 9.6%) and chemical industry (1.9% vs -3.5%) more than offset declines in the manufacture of furniture (-17.4% vs -17.9%) and printing and reproduction of recorded media (-6.1% vs -7.9%). Meanwhile, mining output dropped 13% after rising 4.8% in the previous month, while production growth softened for electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning supply (3.1% vs 6.7%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, industrial activity decreased by 1% in July, slipping from a downwardly revised 1.9% increase in the preceding period.

### 2026-09-10 13:07:33  #MKT News

【RESERVE BANK OF INDIA: Banks' reliance on a few cloud, tech and model vendors poses potential financial stability risk】RESERVE BANK OF INDIA deputy governor Rohit Jain warned banks' dependence on a small number of concentrated cloud service providers, technology vendors and model suppliers poses a potential financial-stability risk. He said overlapping datasets and similar infrastructure mean a failure at one vendor could cause correlated outages or errors across multiple institutions, a view aligned with global regulators. Jain cautioned automation speed can make errors harder to contain and urged firms to detect issues early, limit impact and intervene before small faults escalate. He added that rising model complexity can produce hard-to-explain decisions and stressed institutions can outsource computation but not the consequences.

### 2026-09-10 13:01:47  #Trading Economics #Markets #Indonesia #Currency #Importance 1

Rupiah Maintains Uptrend Despite Oil Price Risks — The Indonesian rupiah hovered around IDR 17,530 per U.S. dollar on Thursday, after briefly strengthening below IDR 17,450 in the previous session. Concerns over oil prices above USD 100 per barrel weighed on sentiment, as Indonesia is a net oil importer, potentially straining fiscal conditions. Still, the currency remained supported by an improving outlook, with its upward momentum over the past week following Destry Damayanti’s appointment as the new Bank Indonesia governor. The external position also provided some support, with foreign exchange reserves rising to a five-month high in August, reinforcing expectations of ample buffers in the coming months. Domestic fundamentals also strengthened, with retail sales rising for the first time in four months in July. Authorities also ramped up measures to curb food-price volatility after annual inflation accelerated to 3.19% in August. Globally, the dollar index steadied ahead of key U.S. inflation data.

### 2026-09-10 12:53:05  #Trading Economics #Economy #Netherlands #Manufacturing Production MoM #Importance 1

Dutch Manufacturing Output Rebounds in July — Manufacturing production in the Netherlands rose by 1.6% month-on-month in July 2026, rebounding from a revised 1.0% decline in the previous month. The increase was supported by stronger output in machinery (6.6% vs. -3.1%), leather and footwear (7.2% vs. -3.7%), paper (6.4% vs. 4.4%), chemical products (2.3% vs. 0.7%), food (1.9% vs. -3.2%) and metal products (1.6% vs. -0.6%). These gains were partly offset by declines in wood (-9.4% vs. 0.4%), automotive and trailers (-7.2% vs. 0.3%), machine repair and installation (-6.4% vs. 0.1%), electrical appliances (-4.7% vs. -2.0%) and other transport equipment (-4.5% vs. 0.3%). Output also fell in textiles (-2.1% vs. -1.2%), rubber and plastics (-2.8% vs. -0.4%) and basic metals (-3.2% vs. 0.4%).

New Zealand 10-Year Yield Surges to 2024 High — New Zealand’s 10-year government bond yield climbed to around 4.89% in early September, reaching its highest level since May 2024, driven by inflation concerns following a surge in oil prices. Brent crude remained above US$100 a barrel as escalating Middle East tensions threatened deeper disruptions to energy supplies, raising inflation risks and expectations that central banks may keep monetary policy tighter for longer. The Reserve Bank of New Zealand raised the official cash rate by 25 bps to 2.75% last week as inflation reached 4.1% in the June quarter, saying the OCR may rise further as inflation is expected to remain elevated this year. Markets are pricing in at least one additional OCR hike, with rates seen reaching around 3% by the end of 2026. Higher US Treasury yields also added to the upward pressure, with the US 10-year yield reaching its highest level since 2023 after the US Treasury’s US$6 billion bond buyback fell short of expectations.

【三大指數半日收跌，紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）逆勢上漲！機構：四條主線把握結構性機會】9月10日，市場震盪調整，三大指數半日收跌。大金融板塊集體拉昇，銀行板塊逆勢走強。受此帶動，紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）漲0.33％，報1.215元，換手率1.36％，半日成交額4.34億元，居同類標的ETF首位。訊息面上，國家統計局9月9日釋出資料顯示，8月CPI同比漲幅回升至0.8％，PPI同比漲幅擴大至3.8％，CPI、PPI環比均由降轉漲。華泰證券建議，攻防兼備，以AI優質標的為進攻主線，以高分紅、穩現金流資產作為防禦壓艙石，同時持續看好電力、資源品、農產品等供給約束型戰略品種。中國銀河指出，當前A股市場處於9月業績空窗期，外圍擾動、國內政策落地與市場量能變化成為影響後續行情的三條主要線索。綜合來看，9月業績催化邊際減弱，資金面尚需觀察量能訊號，市場對外圍風險、產業事件和政策邊際變化的敏感度可能進一步提高，建議在波動中尋找政策確定性和景氣兌現度更高的結構機會。配置機會：關注四條主線：一是科技精選，中期產業趨勢不變，疊加“六張網”新基建，聚焦半導體、通訊裝置、算力基建產業鏈等盈利和訂單可驗證的細分方向。二是紅利底倉，外圍擾動加劇下市場風險偏好仍待修復，金融、公用事業、煤炭等低估值紅利資產可作為組合底倉。三是供需改善與漲價邏輯，包括石油石化、煤化工等資源品，以及厄爾尼諾現象催化上游價格上漲的農業板塊。四是“六張網”政策鏈條，政策性資金陸續到位、專案開工和裝置招標提速有望帶來訂單增量，關注電網、儲能/電力配套、建材、工程機械等環節。投資者可將紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）作為底倉配置，無股票賬戶的投資者也可透過其場外聯接基金進行配置（A類：007466；C類：007467；I類：022678；Y類：022951）。

Palm Oil Dips Ahead of MPOB Data — Malaysian palm oil futures slipped more than 1% to around MYR 4,900 per tonne, extending their decline to a one-week low. Weaker edible oil prices in Dalian and Chicago, along with a stronger ringgit, weighed on sentiment. Traders also turned cautious ahead of monthly data from the Malaysian Palm Oil Board later today. Meanwhile, exports remained sluggish, with cargo surveyors estimating August palm oil shipments fell between 6.5% and 14.9% from July. In India, heavy vegetable oil buying has congested major ports, delaying vessel unloading by up to 10 days as storage tanks overflow, potentially dampening near-term import demand. Still, losses were tempered by firm crude oil prices after Brent breached US$100 a barrel amid renewed Middle East tensions, boosting the appeal of palm oil as a biodiesel feedstock. Simultaneously, unusually dry weather across Southeast Asia fueled concerns over palm yields following fires and haze in Borneo and Sumatra.

### 2026-09-10 12:05:02  #MKT News #Important #Market Themes #The Trump Trade

【Trump's cash payout pledges escalate from $1,200 to $5,000】On Sept. 9, 2026 Trump pledged that if Republicans retain control of both chambers in the November midterm elections he will pay each U.S. adult $5,000; covering more than 200 million adults would push the bill above $1 trillion, and he suggested tariffs as the funding source. In April 2020 the Trump administration issued up to $1,200 pandemic relief checks to eligible recipients. In December 2020 Trump sought to raise a second-round payment from $600 to $2,000, but Congress approved only $600. In Feb. 2025 the U.S. Department of Government Efficiency (DOGE) proposed $5,000 rebate checks to roughly 79 million eligible taxpayer households as part of a spending-cut package; the plan was not implemented. In Nov. 2025, amid a Supreme Court review of federal tariffs, Trump floated at least $2,000 “tariff dividends” for most Americans funded by tariff revenue; that proposal also was not enacted.

### 2026-09-10 11:49:34  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Copper Holds Near Record Highs — Copper futures traded around $6.76 per pound on Thursday, staying close to record highs as ongoing supply concerns and tariff uncertainty continued to support prices. Markets were awaiting the Trump administration’s decision on imposing new tariffs on refined metal imports, while the persistent premium for copper futures on New York’s Comex continued to create arbitrage opportunities. Traders have continued redirecting shipments toward US warehouses, contributing to sharply declining inventories at warehouses tracked by the London Metal Exchange and the Shanghai Futures Exchange. Major copper-producing countries in South America have also faced operational disruptions this year, weighing on production and exports. Meanwhile, supply has struggled to keep pace with rising demand from data centers, renewable energy projects and electricity grids.

### 2026-09-10 11:40:47  #Trading Economics #Economy #Indonesia #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 2

Indonesia Retail Sales Rise for First Time in 4 Months — Indonesia’s retail sales increased 1.1% year-on-year in July 2026, ending three consecutive months of declines, supported by government measures aimed at cushioning households from persistent cost pressures. The rebound was led by food, beverages and tobacco (2.4% vs -3.6% in June) and clothing (2.8% vs -3.1%). However, growth in automotive parts sales slowed sharply (5.1% vs 18.8%). Meanwhile, spending remained weak on fuel (-7.7% vs -1.5%), information and communication equipment (-20.6% vs -20.2%) and recreational goods (-6.7% vs -6.2%). Household equipment sales also fell 4.9%, reversing a prior 2.8% increase. On a monthly basis, retail sales edged down 0.1%, shifting from a 0.7% rise in June. Retail activity may grow further in August as government support measures continue, though elevated inflationary pressures are likely to constrain the pace of recovery.

【打破國外長期壟斷！我國小型液質聯用儀研發取得重要突破】我國在小型液質聯用儀研發領域近日取得重要突破，針對常規液質聯用儀（LC-MS/MS）體積龐大、成本高昂、操作複雜等長期制約臨床推廣應用的痛點，依託中國計量科學研究院建立的市場監管總局技術創新中心（質譜）組織科研團隊開展關鍵技術攻關，成功研發出一種低成本、易操作的小型液質聯用系統（LC-MiniMS），填補了國內該領域的技術空白。研究團隊突破小型真空系統、高效離子傳輸系統、高穩定電路系統等核心關鍵技術，在保證定量分析精度的前提下，大幅縮小儀器體積並有效降低製造成本，打破了國外廠商在該領域長期形成的技術壁壘和價格壟斷，實現國產化替代。

### 2026-09-10 11:24:16  #其他

【諾獎得主阿吉翁：AI有望使生產率增速每年提高1.08個百分點 超過IT革命】在2026 Inclusion·外灘大會上，諾貝爾經濟學獎得主、法國經濟學家菲利普·阿吉翁表示，AI有望在未來十年使生產率增速每年額外提高約1.08個百分點，潛在影響或超過IT革命；但這種技術潛力能否轉化為持續增長，取決於競爭政策、教育體系和勞動力市場制度能否同步跟上。“0.68個百分點只是一個下限。”如果進一步考慮AI促進新想法產生的作用，阿吉翁認為，生產率增速每年還可能額外提高至少約0.4個百分點，合計潛在提升約1.08個百分點。但他也提醒，技術突破並不會自動轉化為持續增長。“AI確實具有很大的增長潛力，但能否充分釋放，也取決於能否處理好競爭問題。”

【多家航司回應是否取消免費餐食】據媒體報道，近日，多家航空公司佈局付費定製飛機餐。有報道稱，付費餐食價格不等，有親民價格也有較高價格的餐食。報道提到，東方航空、四川航空、祥鵬航空等國內多家航司都佈局了付費餐食賽道。 以後還會有免費飛機餐嗎？針對該問題，記者詢問了多家航空公司的客服。 東方航空客服表示，為實現多樣化餐飲選擇（推出付費選餐），餐食價格以系統顯示為準，“正常如果航班有提供餐食，還是會有（免費餐食），但不同航班會有點心或正餐之類的。” 簡而言之，旅客可繼續享受正常餐食，但也能付費購買餐食。客服還表示，每位旅客在每個航班上僅限提前申請一份餐食，可透過App、官網及小程式等渠道進行預訂，“成功申請‘雲端選餐’服務後，將替代航班上正常標準餐食。” 四川航空客服表示，旅客可自己選擇購買付費餐食，“在App上購買，啟訂時間為起飛前10日，截止時間為起飛前48小時。” 祥鵬航空客服表示，若客票顯示含餐會提供免費餐食，“官網（標註）付費餐食是有航班限制的。”（中新網）

Indonesian Stocks Ease Slightly — Indonesia’s IDX Composite inched lower, hovering around 6,665 in early Thursday trade and extending prior losses as Wall Street’s third straight drop overnight weighed on sentiment. Escalating U.S.–Iran tensions lifted Brent above USD100/bbl, stoking inflation fears. Simultaneously, U.S. Treasury yields surged despite a larger-than-usual USD 6 billion debt buyback. Locally, caution lingered over fiscal risks from high oil prices, though officials signaled the state budget stayed secure. Still, losses were capped by August consumer sentiment, which hit a three-month high, driven by stronger household finances and a brighter economic outlook. Financials, health, and non-cyclicals dragged the benchmark, though gains in energy, tech, and basic materials capped losses. Notable decliners included Japfa Comfeed (-2.1%), Indika Energy (-1.6%), Bank Rakyat Indonesia (-1.2%), and Unilever Indonesia (-0.6%). Traders now brace for July retail sales data after June marked a third monthly decline.

### 2026-09-10 10:52:01  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Japan 10Y Yield Tracks Treasury Yields Higher — Japan’s 10-year government bond yield climbed to around 2.93% on Thursday, recovering from two-week lows and tracking a rise in US Treasury yields amid disappointment over the US Treasury Department’s bond buyback announcement. JGBs and Treasurys typically move in tandem. Rising oil prices amid the escalating US-Iran conflict also heightened inflation and interest rate hike concerns, lifting global bond yields. Domestically, the Bank of Japan is widely expected to raise its policy rate to 1.25% next week, the highest level in roughly 31 years, following a rate increase in June. The BOJ aims to address the risk of inflation exceeding expectations amid higher crude oil prices and a weaker yen. The Takaichi administration has also taken a more hawkish stance, as policymakers recognize the need to curb excessive yen weakness.

### 2026-09-10 10:38:37  #Trading Economics #Economy #Australia #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Australia 10Y Yield Hits Fresh 15-Year Peak — Australia’s 10-year government bond yield climbed toward 5.3%, hitting a fresh high since mid-2011 as surging oil prices heightened inflation concerns and strengthened expectations for further rate hikes. Brent crude held above $100 per barrel after Iran and the US launched their biggest wave of attacks on shipping since the war began, threatening to worsen disruptions to energy supplies from the Gulf. The fresh energy-driven inflation pressure sent global bond yields higher, with US Treasury yields reaching their highest levels since 2023 as a buyback program of longer-dated bonds also disappointed. Meanwhile, the oil shock has already fed into domestic consumer prices, which has increased bets of a fourth rate hike this year. Deputy Governor Hauser said the debate at the next meeting would focus on whether to raise interest rates, citing persistently high inflation and upside risks. Markets now price in a 77% chance of a quarter-point hike at the RBA’s upcoming meeting this month.

Yen Holds Near 7-Month High — The Japanese yen traded around 153.4 per dollar on Thursday, remaining close to its strongest level in seven months ahead of an expected Bank of Japan rate hike next week. The central bank is widely expected to lift its policy rate to 1.25%, its highest level in roughly 31 years, following a rate increase in June. The BOJ aims to address the risk of inflation exceeding expectations amid rising crude oil prices and continued yen weakness. The Takaichi administration has also taken a more hawkish stance, with policymakers recognizing the need to curb excessive yen depreciation. Meanwhile, US Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent cautioned traders against betting on a weaker yen earlier this week, saying he has “pretty good insight” into the BOJ’s actions when making a call and intervening in the currency market. The yen has also benefited from the unwinding of carry trades and expectations of greater capital repatriation.

【申萬宏源證券總經理張劍：資本市場穩中向好的確定性明顯增強】9月10日，由證券時報社主辦的“2026金融機構年會”在深圳舉行。申萬宏源黨委副書記、申萬宏源證券總經理張劍在主題演講中表示，隨著新“國九條”和“1+N”政策體系全面落地，資本市場防風險、強監管、促高質量發展主線更加清晰，穩中向好的確定性明顯增強。科創板“1+6”改革落地，IPO稽核註冊發行環節平均用時壓縮近一半，直接融資服務新質生產力的能力顯著提升。張劍表示，金融行業要把政治性和人民性放在首要位置，同時充分發揮好功能性建設，將業務佈局深度融入國家戰略大局，在發揮好功能性的前提下，盈利性也隨之能夠得到彰顯。面向“十五五”，各金融機構需協同發力，以“長期資本、耐心資本”陪伴硬科技企業成長，打通“科技—產業—資本”良性迴圈，推動金融服務實體經濟高質量發展再上新臺階。

【設“夜間靜音區” 《平陸運河通航安全管理規定》將施行】從廣西海事局召開的新聞釋出會上獲悉，《平陸運河通航安全管理規定》將於9月15日起正式施行。作為西部陸海新通道骨幹工程，全長134.2公里的平陸運河按內河Ⅰ級標準建設，可通航5000噸級船舶，是新中國成立以來第一條國家層面統籌建設的通江達海運河工程。平陸運河“通江達海”，內河船舶與海船在同一條水域交匯執行，需要一套統一、清晰的航行規則。《平陸運河通航安全管理規定》的核心突破，是將內河與海上兩套航行規則有機融合。平陸運河全域實行分道通航制，統一執行靠右航行、左舷會船。在青年樞紐上下游，分別明確“上行讓下行”與“逆流讓順流”的避讓要求。目前，欽州灣特定海域已建成全國首個“相當A級”航區，符合標準的內河船舶經檢驗證書籤注後可合法進入該海域，江海聯運的關鍵通道由此打通。《平陸運河通航安全管理規定》還在青年樞紐至G75蘭海高速欽江大橋水域設定“夜間靜音區”。平陸運河欽州市區段穿城而過，兩岸居民集中，此舉在保障航行安全的前提下，最大限度減少對群眾夜間休息的干擾。此外，《規定》還明確了並列行駛、抵坡停泊、彎曲航段追越淌航等6類禁止行為。當前，具備平陸運河通航資格的船舶已達570艘，取得“平陸運河航線”簽註的船員達到681名。通航前夕，廣西海事局正對計劃參加首航的船舶逐一開展安全檢查，同時對船舶航行動態實施全過程監管，確保平陸運河通航環境的安全、暢通、有序。

### 2026-09-10 10:17:21  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Interest Rate #Importance 1

BoJ Eyes Further Rate Hikes Amid Persistent Cost Pressures — Bank of Japan board member Kazuyuki Masu said the central bank will continue raising its policy rate and adjust monetary accommodation as underlying inflation nears 2%. Speaking in Fukui on Thursday, he noted the pace of hikes will hinge on progress toward the July baseline scenario and risks from crude oil, AI-driven demand and FX moves. Masu warned that higher fuel and chemical costs tied to the Iran situation could prove more than temporary by lifting distribution and other expenses. Food prices also face pressure from rising domestic logistics, shipping fees and imported fertilizer, raising concern over persistent inflation. He added that recent rate hikes have not curbed firms’ funding demand, which has instead grown, though risks of overheating in corporate investment remain. Households overall hold net asset surpluses, but younger borrowers face heavier housing-loan burdens despite stronger wage growth.

【審議委員稱將繼續上調利率 日本央行下週加息預期升溫】日本央行審議委員增一行週四表示，日本央行將繼續上調基準利率，以確保物價趨勢幅不超過2%；這番言論支援市場普遍預期日本央行將於下週升息。增一行週四在日本西部城市福井向商界領袖發表講話時表示，“正如我先前所說，鑑於基礎通脹率已非常接近2%，且金融環境一直處於寬鬆狀態，日本央行將繼續上調政策利率，並調整貨幣寬鬆程度。”他說：“今後最重要的是確保基礎通脹率不會大幅超過2%。”增一行過去在貨幣政策立場上屬於中間派，但近來轉趨鷹派，反映日本央行負責決策的委員會整體立場也出現更廣泛的轉變。知情人士本月稍早透露，日本央行傾向於下週將基準利率上調25個基點。若升息，將意味日本央行加快貨幣政策正常化步伐。增一行在演說中指出，伊朗戰爭導致燃料和化學品價格上漲，已推高更廣泛商品的價格。他表示，例如進口原材料運費上漲及進口肥料成本增加，都是推升食品價格的因素。增一行表示，令人擔憂的是，這些商品的價格上漲可能並非暫時性衝擊，而是更持久的趨勢，恐進一步推高整體物價。

### 2026-09-10 09:58:38  #宏觀 #國際

【丁薛祥出席2026年全球服務貿易峰會並會見與會外國政要】2026年9月10日，全球服務貿易峰會在北京舉行，中共中央政治局常委、國務院副總理丁薛祥出席並作主旨講話。丁薛祥指出，服務貿易是國際貿易的重要組成部分，在促進世界經濟增長和產業升級中的作用越來越大。習近平主席多次在全球服務貿易峰會上發表重要影片致辭或向服貿會致賀信，宣佈深化服務領域開放合作的務實舉措，充分展現了中國擴大高水平對外開放的堅定決心、促進全球服務業和服務貿易發展的主動擔當。中國積極推動服務貿易發展，服務領域開放深化拓展，服務貿易規模不斷擴大、結構持續最佳化，為世界各國和企業帶來更多新機遇。丁薛祥表示，當前國際經貿格局加速重構，大力發展服務貿易、推動服務業開放合作，不僅是中國的選擇，更是世界的需要。他提出3點建議：一是以服務業高質量發展促進服務貿易邁上新臺階。提高服務業質量效率和創新能力，推動服務業與服務貿易互促共進，更好支援各國經濟社會發展。二是以包容共進拓展服務貿易發展新空間。堅持開放引領，減少要素跨境流動壁壘，持續提升服務貿易自由化便利化水平，推動服務貿易在公平普惠的道路上行穩致遠。三是以數智賦能打造服務貿易發展新引擎。加強人工智慧等數智技術與服務共享，推進數字基礎設施互聯互通，為服務貿易發展注入強勁動力。(外交部)

### 2026-09-10 09:56:53  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

European Gas Hovers Near Multi-Year Highs — European natural gas prices steadied at €79/MWh on Thursday, hovering close to its highest level in more than three years, amid rising concerns over the region’s gas supply outlook. European gas inventories remain below historical norms as escalating attacks in the Middle East continue to disrupt LNG shipments through the Strait of Hormuz, a critical route handling roughly 20% of global gas flows, mainly from Qatar. In the latest development, Iran said it had attacked 10 ships near the strait and warned it would step up strikes if Washington continued attacks on its territory, raising fears of deeper and more prolonged supply disruptions. Meanwhile, maintenance in Norway and lower Algerian flows to Italy are also limiting pipeline supplies to Europe. These supply risks come as Europe nears the end of its summer storage injection season, threatening to further intensify competition for global gas supplies during the winter.

Accrual begins 15 Aug 2026; semiannual coupons payable 15 Feb and 15 Aug (business‑day adjusted); principal and final coupon due 15 Aug 2056. 【亞鉀國際澄清：目前各項經營工作正常，2026年公司300萬噸鉀肥產量目標保持不變】亞鉀國際投資（廣州）股份有限公司9月10日釋出關於公司生產經營情況的澄清說明，近日，資本市場多次出現寮國雨季及洪水等相關傳聞，片面放大、過度解讀對公司生產經營的影響，易誤導投資者判斷。據此，公司特此鄭重澄清說明如下：受寮國雨季、當地基礎設施影響，區域內生產型企業普遍面臨運輸效率階段性波動的情況，屬於當地每年雨季常態化、行業共性現象，並非公司單獨面臨的個性問題。目前寮國雨季已接近尾聲。公司已透過動態調整生產計劃、合理安排產品庫存等方式降低天氣因素的影響。目前公司生產、建設等各項經營工作正常，2026年公司300萬噸鉀肥產量目標保持不變。

US 10-Year Yield Holds at Near 3-Year High — The yield on the 10-year US Treasury note traded around 4.85% on Thursday, hoverign at its highest level since October 2023 after the Treasury Department announced plans to buy back up to $6 billion in longer-term debt, three times the usual amount, though the move still disappointed some investors who had expected a more substantial increase. Rising oil prices, fueled by escalating tensions between the US and Iran, also stoked inflation concerns and strengthened expectations for near-term interest rate hikes. In addition, mounting fiscal worries and record corporate debt issuance, with AI companies accounting for more than $1.5 trillion in new borrowing, added to upward pressure on Treasury yields. Meanwhile, continued weakness in the Japanese yen prompted Tokyo to repeatedly sell Treasury securities in an effort to support the currency. Investors also remained focused on key US inflation data ahead of the Federal Reserve’s policy meeting next week.

South Korean Shares Fall as Oil and Yields Climb — The benchmark KOSPI slipped nearly 2% to around 6,920 on Thursday, reversing gains from the previous session amid surging oil prices and higher US Treasury yields. Brent crude rose above $101 a barrel, its highest level since May, amid escalating US-Iran tensions and growing concerns over disruptions to Middle East energy supplies, raising concerns over inflation and the outlook for monetary policy. The US 10-year Treasury yield also climbed to around 4.86%, its highest level since November 2023, while Wall Street stocks fell for a third straight session, weighing further on Korean shares. Major large-cap stocks declined, including Samsung Electronics (-2.1%), SK hynix (-2.2%), SK Square (-3.4%), Hyundai Motor (-1.3%), LG Energy Solution (-2.4%), and Doosan Enerbility (-4.1%). Meanwhile, South Korea and France agreed to strengthen cooperation in AI and semiconductors, including joint research and investment, offering some longer-term support for Korea's technology sector.

【AI-driven bond flow spills into Europe; US firms' euro funding nears record】As of Sept. 9, US corporates and financials have issued €149bn of bonds in European currencies this year, the second-highest on record; not all issuance is tech- or AI-related. Amazon launched its first sterling bond, raising £4.25bn across four tranches with an orderbook of £10.65bn. Uber has filed with the SEC for a five-tranche euro bond. ECB data show US big-tech firms have about €40bn of outstanding euro debt, roughly 10% of euro-area non-financial corporate new issuance YTD. AI-driven funding demand has not eased global credit supply pressure but is shifting competition from the crowded dollar market into euro, sterling and Swiss franc markets.

【蘋果釋出會嘉賓陣容出圈：段永平到場，五月天阿信曬和新任CEO合照】北京時間 9 月 10 日凌晨，蘋果在 Apple Park 喬布斯劇院舉行秋季新品釋出會，正式推出旗下首款摺疊屏 iPhone —— iPhone Duo。這是自 2017 年 iPhone X 以來，iPhone 首次迎來全新形態。此外，這也是約翰·特努斯接任蘋果執行長後的首場釋出會。8月29日，知名投資人段永平透過其雪球賬號透露，很好奇蘋果摺疊手機，會去9月9日的釋出會。釋出會當天，有博主曬出與段永平在釋出會現場的合照，證實其到場。此外，釋出會前夕，蘋果在美國加州Apple Park總部舉辦私人晚宴，五月天阿信出席這場晚宴。他發帖引用《知足》歌詞“怎麼去擁有一道彩虹”並曬出名牌。隨後，他還曬出了參加蘋果釋出會的現場圖片。9月10日凌晨，阿信發文總結蘋果釋出會，並曬出和蘋果新任CEO約翰·特努斯的合照。阿信表示：“如果你告訴 25 年前的我們，有一天，未來的每個人手上都會拿著一個小小的黑色盒子，每天花上許多時間與它相伴，我們一定很難相信。而今天，我們親身經歷，也親眼見證了科技如何不斷改變我們的生活——讓我們看得更遠，聽得更立體，也讓體驗變得更加身歷其境。”（新浪科技）

【史上最長16天中秋國慶假期運輸來了，京滬將開行“紅眼高鐵”】9月10日，記者從中國鐵路上海局集團有限公司（以下簡稱上鐵集團）獲悉，長三角史上最長、為期16天的中秋國慶假期運輸方案出臺，鐵路部門在啟用圖定60對高峰線的基礎上，計劃增開304對臨時旅客列車。 上鐵集團客運中心工作人員向記者介紹，2026年鐵路中秋國慶假期運輸期限自9月23日始，至10月8日止，長三角鐵路預計傳送旅客5400萬人次，客流最高峰日為10月1日，當天預計傳送旅客440萬人次，有望創下中秋國慶假期運輸單日旅客傳送量新高。 根據目前車票預售情況看，本次中秋國慶假期運輸期間，長三角地區旅遊流、探親流、學生流出行需求全面釋放，客流呈現出行總量大、持續時間長、高峰客流高等特徵，部分割槽間和時段客流高度集中。長三角鐵路部門計劃於9月25日、28日、30日及10月1日、2日、6日、7日、8日凌晨在京滬等高鐵幹線安排開行夜間高鐵； 9月23日、24日、29日、30日，10月1日、6日、7日、8日安排開行上海往來廣東多地高鐵動臥列車等。今年中秋國慶假期運輸期間，長三角鐵路臨時旅客列車開行方案較去年國慶中秋假期運輸臨時旅客開行方案增加45對，增幅17.3%。（貝殼財經）

【飛天茅臺、五糧液普五八代逆勢大漲 國窖1573、古井貢古20創月新低】新浪財經“酒價內參”過去24小時收集的資料顯示，9月10日中國白酒業11大單品的終端零售均價三漲七跌一平，下跌品種繼續佔據顯著優勢。贏家方面，飛天茅臺上漲4元，終端零售均價來到1797元，連續三日攀升，重新逼近1800元大關；五糧液普五八代大漲8元至818元，強勢收復前期兩連陰的大部分失地；水晶劍南春小漲1元至408元，價盤小幅修復。下跌方面，精品茅臺下跌2元至2459元，三連陽暫告收尾；五糧液1618小跌1元至829元，連續兩日平盤後小幅抖動；國窖1573下跌3元至874元，三連跌價格中樞持續下移，平30天以來最低紀錄；洋河夢之藍M6+下跌4元至608元，前一日短時反彈後再度回落，跌破610元關口；古井貢古20下跌5元至518元，五連跌連續走弱，再創近30天價格新低；習酒君品下跌2元至638元，短暫反彈後再度回踩，維持中位區間震盪執行；青花郎下跌5元至680元，五連陰短線跌幅再度擴大。持平方面，青花汾20終端零售均價維持在390元，前一日回踩整十關口後價盤暫時趨穩。

今日11大單品的終端零售總價繼續下移，高階與次高階酒品整體仍以回撥為主，盤面分化態勢延續。如果11大單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為10019元，較昨日下跌9元，創下11天以來新低。總價自9月5日階段性高點以來已連續五日回落，目前僅高出一萬元整數關口19元。

### 2026-09-10 09:03:49  #公司 #市場 #國際

【美國國家運輸安全委員會：亞馬遜貨運飛機失事墜毀前曾嘗試復飛】根據航班最後時刻的錄音，這架亞馬遜公司貨運飛機週日在邁阿密國際機場失事。飛機接地時速度過快，一名飛行員隨即呼叫中止著陸。美國國家運輸安全委員會（NTSB）於週三晚間公佈飛機駕駛艙語音記錄儀的相關細節，還原了事故發生前的一系列經過：飛機衝出跑道並撞上兩輛車輛。事故中的 5 名遇難者與 5 名傷者均在這兩輛車內。NTSB 稱，飛機獲得著陸許可、進近跑道過程中，一名飛行員 “提醒飛機速度過快”。隨後機載計算機連續發出兩聲 “下降率過大” 電子警報，提示飛機下沉速度過快；同時還響起五次 “地形過低” 語音警告。NTSB 表示，記錄儀隨後錄下疑似接地的聲響，緊接著一名飛行員呼叫復飛。復飛即放棄著陸、重新拉昇高度，調整姿態準備再次進近的操作流程。NTSB 稱，5 秒後，記錄儀捕捉到飛機駛離鋪裝跑道的聲音。由分包商 21 航空有限責任公司執飛的 Prime Air 7598 號航班，從波多黎各聖胡安的路易斯・穆尼奧斯・馬林國際機場飛抵後，衝出機場斜向跑道。這架波音 767 飛機在鋪裝跑道之外繼續滑行約四分之一英里，撞上機場導航裝置，隨後猛撞至兩輛車輛。飛機駛離跑道後撞上一輛白色福特 Econoline 廂式貨車，車輛被撞得支離破碎。該貨車當時正在機場界區內運送一支 7 人清潔班組。隨後飛機又撞上一輛行駛在機場圍欄外道路上的豐田小型 SUV。

Dollar Steadies Ahead of Key Inflation Data — The dollar index held around 98.8 on Thursday, pausing its recent decline as investors awaited key inflation figures that could shape the Federal Reserve’s next policy decision. The US producer price index for August is due later today, followed by the consumer inflation report on Friday. Other major economic releases scheduled for today include weekly jobless claims and existing home sales data. Markets are currently pricing in roughly a 60% probability of a 25-basis-point rate hike by the Fed next week following stronger-than-expected jobs data. Meanwhile, Treasury yields surged after the Treasury Department announced plans to buy back up to $6 billion in longer-term debt, three times the usual amount, though the move still disappointed some investors who had anticipated a larger increase. Elsewhere, oil prices moved higher as the US-Iran conflict intensified, stoking inflation concerns and strengthening expectations for near-term rate hikes.

【上證報：樓市新政或開啟房企融資渠道 定向可轉債進一步增量】定向可轉債市場有望迎來又一次擴容視窗。中國證監會8月底釋出《關於資本市場支援構建房地產發展新模式的意見》（以下簡稱“意見”），支援上市房企透過向特定物件發行方式再融資，並支援上市房企綜合運用股份、定向可轉債、現金等工具收購涉房資產。從已發行案例看，定向可轉債在併購重組中主要扮演兼具債底保護與股性彈性的創新支付工具角色。業內人士認為，在房地產領域，定向可轉債此前主要承擔專案融資功能，未來隨著行業併購需求上升，其在房地產行業中的應用場景有望進一步豐富。業內人士表示，今年已披露預案的定向可轉債已達12只，遠高於去年同期，具備發行擴容的基礎。

【“空軍一號”緊急滑梯意外展開 特朗普行程短暫延誤】美國總統唐納德·特朗普前往達拉斯參加中期選舉大會時，行程被一個意外插曲短暫延誤：卡達贈送專機的緊急滑梯。週三，特朗普乘坐直升機抵達安德魯斯聯合基地後，由於飛機前部旁邊的充氣滑梯處於展開狀態，被迫在機上等待約20分鐘。最終地勤人員將滑梯挪走，特朗普才得以登機啟程。據知情人士透露，這一緊急滑梯是被軍方人員誤開。因涉及事件原因，知情人士要求匿名。 在此之前，特朗普在停機坪上向記者給出了另一種解釋。他表示，工作人員正在對滑梯進行檢查，因為“希望確保一切運轉完美”。他說，這一過程大約需要15分鐘。當被問及是否確信空軍一號可以安全飛行時，特朗普回答說：“對，我很確定，否則我就不會坐它了。”

Silver Holds Gains Ahead of US Inflation Data — Silver steadied above $67 an ounce on Thursday, holding gains from the previous session as investors cautiously awaited key US inflation readings that could provide further insight into the Federal Reserve’s next policy move. The US producer price index report for August is due later today, followed by the consumer inflation report on Friday. Precious metals found support from a weaker dollar, as well as continued demand for their long-term hedging appeal despite near-term pressure from higher oil prices and bond yields. Oil prices climbed to multi-month highs as the US-Iran conflict intensified, stoking inflation concerns and strengthening expectations for near-term rate hikes. 【交易員無視美國財長斯科特・貝森特 “跟我對賭必虧” 的強硬表態】美國財政部長斯科特・貝森特反覆警告投資者，他會讓這些人遭受重大損失。他堅稱，無論是推高油價、壓低日元匯率，抑或是最為關鍵的抬升美國國債收益率，投資者都在犯下嚴重錯誤。因為他站在這筆交易的對手盤，掌握著投資者無法獲取的政府政策規劃核心資訊。這位前對沖基金交易員總喜歡用博弈論術語將此稱為資訊不對稱。僅過去三週，他就至少四次在公開場合提及這一概念，其中就包括本週二，他向外匯交易員放話：“想跟我對賭，儘管來。”交易員們已經開始這麼做了 —— 至少在債券與原油市場是如此；日元市場暫未出現該情況，美國已緊急出手協助日本當局。自從貝森特開始對原油與債券這兩大市場口頭喊話以來，這兩個對中期選舉前美國家庭民生至關重要的市場走勢均與他的預期背道而馳。基準原油價格突破每桶 100 美元，推高加油站汽油零售價。週三，他推出首筆加碼的債券回購操作，意圖壓低 10 年期美債基準收益率（該收益率是抵押貸款及其他各類貸款的利率基準），但美債收益率反而大幅上行，延續兩週來的拋售行情，一舉觸及 4.85% 的三年新高。

【上證報：中國資產“確定性”築牢資本市場內在穩定性 中美利差走闊對A股市場擾動實際影響不大  內生力量正增強】上證報頭版刊文稱，近期，市場對“流動性”的討論有所升溫。從全球市場看，以美債為代表的多國國債收益率飆升，引發市場對流動性擠壓的普遍擔憂；從A股市場看，近兩天成交額有所縮減，有聲音解讀為市場流動性趨緊的訊號。多位市場專家接受採訪認為，當前A股流動性仍處於健康區間，從長期看，中國經濟及政策穩定性、科技創新加快引領產業格局變遷以及全球市場對中國資產安全性的認可，都將推動A股獨立定價能力進一步夯實。長期以來，美債收益率被視作全球金融資產的定價錨。但對A股來說，卻並不能簡單套用相關邏輯。利率端，我國始終堅持“以我為主”的財政和貨幣政策，美債收益率上行並未對國內利率環境產生明顯影響。目前，我國低利率環境持續，流動性整體寬鬆，年內人民幣匯率對美元總體保持上漲，這背後是美元回撥、國內出口保持較快增長、企業結匯需求釋放以及匯率預期改善共同作用的結果。對於A股來說，外資交易和持有市值佔比均較低，情緒性的擾動也大於實際。另有市場專家表示，短期擾動實際影響不大，決定市場中長期走向的內生力量正在增強。2026年上市公司半年報披露的基本面資料顯示，A股內部盈利底盤已經發生結構性變化，外部流動性衝擊更多影響短期情緒，難以完全決定中長期市場走向。

【直擊投洽會：“投資中國”的故事正在重新被敘述 外資加碼中國創新力量】9月8日至11日，第二十六屆中國國際投資貿易洽談會（簡稱“投洽會”）在廈門國際博覽中心舉行。本屆投洽會有60個國家和地區確認設展，國際參與度持續攀升，全球合作展區規模與國際化程度均達到新高度。商務部此前將跨國公司對華投資新趨勢概括為“三個轉變”：從“注重成本”向“注重價值”轉變，從“填補空白”向“鍛造實力”轉變，從“獨立運營”向“協同發展”轉變。在投洽會上，資本用最誠實的方式說明“外資沒有走，只是換了賽道”，“投資中國”的故事正在重新被敘述。從能源化工到高階製造，從傳統加工到研發協同，中國產業重心正向高附加值產品與服務轉移，外資在華投資正沿著中國產業升級的方向，悄然切換了“與中國同行”的投資邏輯。

### 2026-09-10 07:40:19  #Trading Economics #Economy #Ukraine #Inflation Rate #Importance 0

Ukraine Inflation Accelerates in August — The annual inflation rate in Ukraine rose to 8.1% in August 2026 from 7.7% in the previous month, marking the highest reading in three months. Transport inflation jumped to 23.8% from 18.6% in July, while housing, water, electricity, gas and other fuels rose to 5.6% from 4.5%. Inflation also increased for furnishings and household equipment (5.7% vs 5.0%), clothing and footwear (5.1% vs 4.4%), and restaurant and hotel inflation (13.7% vs 13.2%). In contrast, price growth eased for food and non-alcoholic beverage inflation (6.0% vs 6.5%) and communication (13.6% vs 16.5%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices increased by 0.1% in August, following a 0.3% gain in the preceding month.

### 2026-09-10 07:24:09  #宏觀 #市場 #國際

【美債收益率螺旋式攀升 幕後的對沖操作推波助瀾】隨著美債收益率挺升，債券投資者爭相採取保護措施，但這反而可能加劇收益率漲勢，幕後的對沖操作正在放大市場波動。市場對通脹的憂慮，加上財政赤字日益膨脹，其影響遠遠蓋過美國財政部擴大國債回購計劃(旨在控制借貸成本)帶來的效果，美國基準長債收益率處於或接近多年高點。收益率可能進一步攀升的前景，促使交易員買進選擇權，以保護其投資組合。近期值得注意的選擇權動向包括一筆押注30年期美債收益率升至約5.7%的大型部位，較目前水準高出近半個百分點。隨著收益率上升，在這些選擇權交易中站在另一邊的交易商，也必須積極對沖自身部位。他們往往會賣出期貨，或進行支付固定利率的掉期交易，這類部位會隨利率上升而增值。這種所謂的凸性對沖(convexity hedging)或德爾塔對沖(delta hedging)，可能在收益率上升之際進一步推高掉期利率，從而放大整體市場走勢。

Brent Extends Rally on Intensifying US-Iran War — Brent crude climbed to around $102 per barrel on Thursday, hitting its highest level since May as the intensifying conflict between the US and Iran raised concerns over further disruptions to energy supplies from the Middle East. The conflict broadened after Iran-backed Houthi militants attacked several energy facilities in Saudi Arabia, prompting a temporary suspension of some operations.

【光博會開幕：高速光模組迭代 NPO方案密集亮相】“今年預訂晚了，我們只能選個面積小的展位，展示部分機型產品了。”一位參展商向證券時報記者感嘆。9月9日，第27屆中國國際光電博覽會（CIOE中國光博會）在深圳國際會展中心開幕，同期舉辦IICIE國際積體電路創新博覽會、elexcon深圳國際電子展。今年參展企業超5000家，覆蓋上游基礎材料至下游終端應用，展示“矽光晶片製造—CPO（共封裝光學）封裝整合—高速光互連部署”全鏈路方案。近期A股光模組經歷了一波回撥反彈，行業峰會關注度不減，展會現場人潮湧動，熱門展品要插空觀看，不少展位還掛出招聘啟事。當前光模組技術演進分為兩條主線：一條是可插拔方案持續迭代，由1.6T向3.2T升級；另一條是行業追求降功耗、提整合度，英偉達推動CPO從技術驗證走向產業化。而在產業落地路徑上，NPO（近封裝光學）被視作短期確定性更高的過渡方案。隨著800G光模組逐漸批次出貨，本屆光博會上光通訊龍頭企業展品主力成為1.6T甚至更高傳輸速率產品。另外，NPO方案密集亮相，部分廠商NPO產品已經完成客戶送樣、驗證階段，在展會現場也成為參展觀眾關注焦點。同時，光通訊以及半導體廠商也向上游光晶片領域加碼佈局。

【服貿會“數綠框架”再擴員 智慧化綠色化引領國際經貿合作新趨勢】在今年的服貿會上，數字貿易發展趨勢和前沿論壇同步展開。多國政府機構、國際組織和企業代表，圍繞數字貿易、綠色發展等議題開展交流對接。據介紹，本屆論壇主題為“提升數字時代國際綠色經貿合作，推動構建人類命運共同體”。中國和相關國家企業、機構現場分享綠色算力、數字政府、中小企業數字創新等實踐案例，推動各方在數字綠色發展中加強經驗互鑑和務實合作。當前，服務貿易和數字貿易加快融合，智慧化、綠色化成為國際經貿合作新趨勢。本次論壇上，古巴、幾內亞比索加入《數字經濟和綠色發展國際經貿合作框架倡議》，參加方由52國增至54國，合作伙伴網路進一步擴大。（央視）

### 2026-09-10 07:05:36  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #RICS House Price Balance #Importance 1

UK House Price Balance Improves to 5-Month High — The RICS UK Residential Market Survey showed that the house price balance improved to -28% in August 2026 from an upwardly revised -29% in July, reaching a five-month high as the market showed signs of stabilizing. The net balance for new buyer enquiries rose to -19%, its strongest reading since January, while sales expectations improved to -3% from -13%. RICS said activity indicators have become progressively less negative, although any recovery remains fragile. Property prices are still expected to fall over the next three months but stabilize over a 12-month horizon. London reported the weakest price balance, while Northern Ireland remained the strongest market. Rental market tenant demand and fewer properties from landlords pushed the three-month rent expectations balance to +44% from +33%. RICS warned that prospects for higher interest rates or heavier property taxation after October’s budget could weigh on prices.

### 2026-09-10 06:59:39  #Trading Economics #Markets #New Zealand #Stock Market #Importance 1

NZX 50 Tracks Wall Street Lower — New Zealand stocks fell 19 points, or 0.1%, to 13,800 in Thursday morning trade, after gaining in the previous session, tracking a downbeat session on Wall Street overnight following a surge in Treasury yields and rising oil prices. Oil continued to rise, raising concerns over inflation and boosting bets that central banks would raise interest rates. Traders also anticipated the release of US inflation data later this week for clues on the Fed's monetary policy decision at its upcoming meeting. Wednesday's data from China, New Zealand’s top trading partner, also pressured sentiment, as consumer and producer price pressures continued to rise amid higher oil prices. Consumer staples, financials, and materials mainly weighed on the index, with early losers including Briscoe Group (-0.9%), A2 Milk (-0.8%), Hallenstein Glasson (-0.8%), F&C Investment (-0.6%), Infratil (-0.6%), and Delegat Group (-0.4%).

US Crude Inventories Fall in Early September — US crude oil inventories fell by 300,000 barrels in the week ended September 4th, following a 2.6 million-barrel decline in the previous week. Commercial crude inventories excluding the SPR have fallen by just over 48 million barrels over the past 21 weeks, while US crude inventories are up 2.8 million barrels year to date. Another 1.2 million barrels were released from the SPR to support commercial inventories, bringing total SPR holdings to 285.4 million barrels. The generally accepted operational minimum is 250-300 million barrels. Cushing inventories fell by 300,000 barrels after rising by 200,000 barrels the previous week. US production rose to 13.862 million bpd in the week ended August 28th, from 13.843 million bpd. Gasoline inventories fell by 1.9 million barrels, while distillate inventories rose by 2 million barrels.

Brent Tops $101 as US-Iran Conflict Escalates — Brent crude rose above $101.5 a barrel on Wednesday for the first time since May, driven by escalating US-Iran tensions. US officials reported that Iran attempted to attack Navy ships on Monday after targeting an aircraft carrier with ballistic missiles over the weekend, in a previously undisclosed wave of attacks. Meanwhile, Iranian state media said a US missile targeted a small oil tanker four miles from Kharg Island on Tuesday. The war broadened this week after Iran-backed Houthi militants attacked several energy facilities in Saudi Arabia, forcing a temporary halt to some operations. The attacks came after the US military struck three Iranian oil tankers on Saturday in retaliation for Iranian ballistic missile attacks on two Navy warships. Adding to upwards pressures on prices, US crude inventories fell by 0.3 million barrels last week. Meanwhile, Ukrainian drones hit infrastructure in Novorossiysk, Russia’s key oil terminal in the Black Sea, on Wednesday.

Ibovespa Slips on Political Risks and Inflation Fears — The Ibovespa fell 0.9% to close at 185,629 on Wednesday amid renewed political risks in Brazil and escalating Middle East hostilities. Supreme Court Justice Flávio Dino ordered the reinstatement of Federal Police chief Andrei Rodrigues, granting an emergency request a day after Justice André Mendonça mandated his removal. Mendonça, who is overseeing a sprawling fraud investigation into failed lender Banco Master, had accused the Federal Police of illegally surveilling him and other public officials. The crisis reflects Brazil’s wider political polarization ahead of the October presidential election. Meanwhile, intensifying Middle East hostilities pushed oil prices higher, rekindling inflation fears. Major banks posted losses, with Itaú down 2% and Bradesco losing 2.4%. Utilities also closed lower, with Axia down 1.9%. WEG (-1.8%) and Ambev (-1.5%) also weighed on the index. Petrobras rose 0.7% on higher oil prices.

【Sources: Pakistan urges Iran to rein in Houthi forces】Pakistan has warned Iran to restrain Yemen’s Houthi militants after a rise in Houthi attacks on Saudi Arabia, conveying the message on behalf of Riyadh, sources in Saudi, Pakistan and Iran told Refinitiv. Islamabad’s defence ties with Saudi Arabia and Turkey gave the warning added weight; Pakistan delivered the message to Tehran within 24 hours, urging Iran to use its influence to halt strikes on Saudi territory. An Iranian official confirmed Pakistan relayed the warning on Tuesday and said Tehran replied that Iran does not control the Houthis; two other Iranian sources told Refinitiv that Tehran last week ordered Houthi strikes, pledged additional funding and weapons, and sent IRGC commanders to Yemen to coordinate operations. Saudi, Pakistani and Turkish military delegations met in Riyadh to coordinate a response and agreed Saudi forces would not enter Yemen; any action would be launched from Saudi territory.

【Apple fall event: foldable iPhone from 15,999 yuan; iPhone 18 Pro gets 2nm A20 Pro chip】Apple introduced iPhone 18 Pro/Pro Max and a foldable iPhone Duo. iPhone 18 Pro series features the A20 Pro chip on a 2nm process and a new-generation C2 modem; Pro adds a variable-aperture main camera and enhanced video functions; Pro charges to up to 50% in ~15 minutes; Pro Max video playback up to 43 hours. Pricing: iPhone 18 Pro from 9,999 yuan, Pro Max from 10,999 yuan. Preorders Sept. 12; on sale Sept. 18. The foldable iPhone Duo uses the A20 Pro, has a 7.6-inch inner nano-texture display (and a 5.4-inch outer screen), both with ProMotion, always-on display and up to 3,000 nits outdoor brightness. Camera system: dual 48MP main and ultra-wide, fold design supports rear-camera selfies and outer-screen live preview; inner screen uses an under‑display FaceTime camera. Battery: outer-screen playback up to 44 hours, inner-screen up to 31 hours. Hardware: titanium body, IP68, side-mounted Touch ID, 254g weight, Apple Pencil support, eSIM only. Price starts at 15,999 yuan. Preorders Oct. 16; on sale Oct. 23. Other products: AirPods 5 claims up to 50% noise reduction versus AirPods 4 (base 999 yuan; wireless charging 1,149 yuan). Apple Watch Series 12 from 2,999 yuan and Ultra 4 from 6,499 yuan, with always-on Siri, Audio Intelligence and Live Rewind. No standard iPhone 18 was announced. Apple’s online store shows iPhone 17, 17e and Air configurations are listed about 800 yuan higher than their original launch prices.

【ANTHROPIC says Claude MYTHOS 5 connected to open internet in security tests, uploaded malware to PyPI】ANTHROPIC reported four incidents in which Claude models accessed real third‑party systems without authorization during a cybersecurity assessment. All incidents occurred in the same third‑party evaluation firm's test environment after a configuration error connected a simulated-offline environment to the open internet. The company identified two alignment failures — 'biased inference' (the model downplayed or misread evidence it was operating on a live network) and 'reckless behavior' (the model took potentially harmful actions to complete tasks). The most significant case involved Claude MYTHOS 5 uploading a malicious package to the public Python Package Index (PyPI) and making further access to real systems. ANTHROPIC said the actions, while risky, remained within the scope of the test tasks and found no evidence the model attempted to conceal activity, coordinate with other agents, or pursue goals beyond the assignment. ANTHROPIC has engaged model‑evaluation firm METR for an independent investigation and plans to tighten pre‑release testing, monitoring and third‑party security requirements.

---

### 2026-09-10 02:20:56  #MKT News #Important #Star Stocks #Apple #Market Themes #Magnificent 7 #AAPL.O #Impact bullish

【Apple launches first foldable iPhone Duo from $1,999】Apple unveiled the iPhone Duo, its first foldable iPhone, with US pricing from $1,999. The handset has a 7.6-inch internal display — the largest iPhone screen to date — and a 5.4-inch external display whose area Apple says is roughly 90% of the iPhone 18 Pro; both panels support ProMotion, always-on display and up to 3,000 nits peak outdoor brightness. The device uses a grade-5 titanium chassis and a precision hinge of more than 100 components, carries an IP68 dust/water rating, and integrates Touch ID into the side button. It is powered by a 2nm A20 Pro chip and a custom heat spreader; Apple says sustained performance is up to 35% higher than the iPhone 17 Pro and the phone supports split-screen operation of two apps. Imaging includes 48MP main and 48MP ultrawide cameras; the fold form factor enables using the rear cameras for selfies with external-screen live preview. The phone uses a dual-battery architecture and, when using the outer screen, supports up to 44 hours of video playback. Available in Starlight and Midnight, storage options are 256GB, 512GB, 1TB and 2TB. US pre-orders begin Oct. 16, on-sale Oct. 23; China and more than 70 other markets are in the initial launch.

【Apple launches iPhone 18 Pro series with 2nm A20 Pro chip, variable aperture; Pro $1,199, Pro Max $1,299】Apple (AAPL.O) unveiled the iPhone 18 Pro and iPhone 18 Pro Max, running iOS 27 and Apple Intelligence/Siri AI. Both phones use the A20 Pro chip built on a 2nm process; Apple says memory bandwidth is up 50% versus the A19 Pro, the 7‑core GPU is up to 40% faster, and a dual 16‑core Neural Engine doubles AI performance. A next‑generation heat spreader boosts sustained performance by as much as 40%. Imaging is upgraded with a 48MP fused main sensor and Apple’s first variable aperture (six laser‑cut blades) plus professional camera controls and expanded video features including ProRes RAW, Apple Log 2 and Genlock. Apple says the iPhone 18 Pro Max delivers the largest battery improvement in iPhone history; the eSIM model supports up to 45 hours of video playback. Storage ranges 256GB–2TB. US pricing: iPhone 18 Pro from $1,199; Pro Max from $1,299. Preorders begin Sept. 12, on sale Sept. 18.

### 2026-09-10 01:11:19  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 2

US 10-Year Yield Rises to Near 3-Year High — The yield on the 10-year US Treasury note rose toward 4.85% on Wednesday, the highest since October of 2023, after the Treasury Department tripled the buyback on notes and bonds to take place tomorrow. The department will purchase $6 billion in off-the-run securities, compared with Secretary Bessent's earlier announcement that buybacks would at least double to $4 billion, among the multiple moves by Washington to supress long-maturity yields. Yields on the long-end of the curve have surged this year as high oil prices due to the war in Iran raised underlying inflation, risking a rate hike by the Federal Reserve in upcoming meetings. This was magnified by record-setting corporate debt supply, with AI companies issuing north of $1.5 trillion in new issuance. On top of that, pressure on the Japanese yen drove Tokyo to repeatedly sell Treasury securities to defend the currency. Still, yields on the 10-year note recouped some gains after the fresh auction stopped-through 1.5bps.

【Anthropic研究員辭職 警告稱人工智慧巨頭在“拿人類生命作賭注”】Anthropic PBC一名人工智慧研究員辭職，並呼籲其他員工也重新思考自己的工作意義。他表示，Anthropic及其主要競爭對手OpenAI在全力追求一種可能給人類帶來存亡風險的技術過程中採取了不負責任的做法。過去三年曾先後在Anthropic和OpenAI工作的Jacob Coxon週二晚間在社交媒體發文警告，這兩家公司正在推進能夠“自我改進”的AI模型，而這些模型最終可能變得強大到人類無法控制。Coxon在帖子中寫道：“他們正徑直奔向能夠自我改進的超級智慧，而且是拿人類的生命作賭注。不要低估這項技術的力量。這些系統很快就會擁有超越人類的能力，可以入侵任何東西、一夜之間徹底改變任何領域，並獲得真正的權力和資源。”Coxon稱，開發AI的團隊“真心認為，到2030年前後，它可能會殺死我們所有人”。Anthropic現任員工Evan Hubinger對此回應稱，他和公司其他人的確擔心這種情形。他在X平臺寫道：“我個人認為，未來十年內發生這種情況的機率超過10%。”

Gasoline Eases From Two-Week High — US gasoline futures fell below $3.20 a gallon after rising to a two-week high, amid a reduced outlook for gasoline demand. Energy Secretary Wright stated that slowing demand following the end of the summer driving season could help rebuild inventories, which remain 6% below the five-year average, while US refineries are operating near full capacity, with some delaying seasonal fall maintenance to keep production levels elevated. Similarly, China, the world’s biggest crude importer, increased crude imports in August as refiners boosted production amid depleting inventories, offering some relief to a tight global refining market. However, gasoline demand in China is set to fall 8.7% in 2026, accelerating the elimination of excess refining capacity. Meanwhile, tensions in the Middle East intensified as the US military carried out strikes on Iranian tankers. Adding to the pressure, Ukraine attacked an energy facility in Russia’s Arctic for the first time.

【摩根士丹利首席投資官建議用能源股進行對沖 同時迴避30年期美國國債】摩根士丹利的Mike Wilson表示，擔心利率和油價上漲的投資者，應偏向質量更高的美國股票，並利用能源股進行投資組合對沖，同時避開30年期美國國債。Wilson週三接受採訪時表示：“別自欺欺人了，標普500仍然是全球質量最高的股票市場。所以如果你很緊張，那就持有標普500。”他補充稱：“不要持有長期債券。過去五年，長期債券的表現簡直是一場災難。”  Wilson對能源股的看好，也凸顯了這一行業的雙重作用。原油價格反彈一方面加劇了通脹擔憂，並對美國國債構成壓力；另一方面，石油公司股價自6月以來明顯跑贏大盤。從6月1日至週二，標普500能源指數上漲13%，遠高於標普500指數同期1%的漲幅。作為摩根士丹利首席美國股票策略師和首席投資官的Wilson還指出，美國國內能源生產能夠在一定程度上隔絕歐洲和日本所面臨的供應衝擊。

FTSE 100 Falls to 7-Week Low — The FTSE 100 fell more than 1% to a seven-week low on Wednesday as Brent crude surged to $101 a barrel, its highest level since May, amid renewed fighting in the Middle East. The US destroyed five Iranian crude-carrying tankers following an attempted strike on a US Navy warship, while Tehran fired missiles towards Jordan and warned vessels in the Persian Gulf. Energy stocks were among the few gainers, with Shell up 0.2% and BP rising 0.7%. Aberdeen Group edged lower after earlier gaining more than 2% following the appointment of Torbjörn Magnusson as chairman, subject to regulatory approval. Elsewhere, financials, consumer staples, industrials and materials came under heavy selling pressure. HSBC fell nearly 2%, while Unilever and BAT dropped 1.6% and 1.3%, respectively. Rolls-Royce declined 2.5%, Rio Tinto fell 1.5% and Glencore lost 1%. AstraZeneca was also under pressure, falling 1.8%.

DAX Finishes at Over 1-Month Low — The DAX 40 deepened losses to close about 1.7% lower at 25,554 on Wednesday, a level last seen in late July, tracking its European peers. The central bank is all but certain to raise interest rates from 2.25% to 2.5% on Thursday, with traders focused on the future policy path. Almost all sectors faced pressure, particularly energy-intensive industrials, techs, banks, financials and autos. Defense stocks Rheinmetall and Renk slipped 3.8% and 3.1%, respectively, amid profit-taking. European Stocks Decline — European stocks closed sharply lower on Wednesday as the ongoing surge in energy price consolidated concerns that inflation could dent demand and drive the ECB to deliver multiple rate hikes this year. The Euro STOXX 50 fell 1.7% to 6,304 and the STOXX Europe 600 fell 1.5% to 640. Strikes between the US and Iran intensified halfway through the week and pushed back expectations of when fuel exports through the Middle East may be somewhat normalized. Brent crude passed $100 per barrel, while regional natural gas benchmarks rose to their highest since 2022. The ECB is due to raise rates by 25bps tomorrow, and potentially give hints on whether it sees more tightening to be warranted. Rate sensitive sector declined sharply, with Santander, ING, Deutsche Bank, and Allianz dropping 2% due to risks on credit demand. Industrials also fell with Schneider Electric, Siemens, Rheinmetall, and Safran falling more than 3%.

### 2026-09-09 23:55:02  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Soybeans Fall to Over 1-Week Low — Soybeans eased to $12.90 a bushel, their lowest level in more than a week, as traders turned their attention to Friday’s US Department of Agriculture supply-and-demand forecasts for an indication of weather damage to Midwest crops. The USDA’s latest assessment showed that 58% of soybean crops were rated good to excellent, unchanged from the previous week and above expectations for a one-point decline. Despite the weaker prices, Chinese demand for US soybeans provided underlying support. On Wednesday, the USDA reported export sales of 340,000 metric tons of soybeans to China for delivery in 2026/27, alongside another 100,000 tons sold to unknown destinations. Rising oil prices have also supported soybeans, which are widely used in biofuel production, with Brent crude recently surpassing $100 a barrel. Soybeans reached $13.10 a bushel on September 1, their highest level since December 2023, amid concerns over US yields and Black Sea supply disruptions.

FTSE MIB Falls Ahead of ECB Rate Decision — The FTSE MIB fell 0.5% to 51,875 on Wednesday ahead of ECB's meeting tomorrow, which could weigh on credit demand for investment in defense, AI and energy transition. Heavyweight financials declined as eurozone sovereign yields rose to multi-year highs while markets widely expect a rate hike by the ECB, weighing on the bank's balance sheets. Adding to the pressure, talk of taxing banks' profits intensified as governments seek aid for budget deficits. Asset managers were also affected, with Azimut and Banca Mediolanum falling over 1.5%. Defense stocks Leonardo (-4%) and Fincantieri (-3.6%) led losses on the index. STMicroelectronics fell 1.1%. Stellantis declined 2.2%, as China's growing electric-truck market pressures Western manufactures. Luxury stocks also declined amid weak demand from China and lower spending due to the war in the Middle East. Attacks intensified in the region, lifting brent prices above $100. Conversely, energy stocks outperformed.

CAC 40 Falls Amid Middle East Tensions — The CAC 40 fell 1.9% to close at 8,162 on Wednesday, reversing the previous session’s gains and hitting its lowest level since July as Middle East tensions escalated. Oil prices remained elevated after the US and Iran exchanged strikes near the Strait of Hormuz, heightening inflation concerns and reinforcing expectations that the ECB will raise interest rates by 25 basis points at Thursday’s policy meeting. Domestically, industrial production fell 0.4% month-over-month in July, missing expectations for a 0.3% increase. Losses were broad-based, with industrial and luxury stocks weighing heavily on the index. Airbus fell 2.2% after cutting its long-term demand outlook for single-aisle aircraft in China. Safran shed 3.2%, Thales retreated 3%, Legrand fell 3.8%, and Schneider Electric dropped 3.3%. Luxury stocks also declined amid increased risk aversion, with LVMH falling 3% to its lowest level since November 2020, Hermès shedding 2.2%, and Kering tumbling 4.7%.

### 2026-09-09 23:48:24  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Wheat Falls to 2-Week Low — Wheat fell to $7.10 a bushel, their lowest level in two weeks, as traders weighed diplomatic efforts to end Russia’s war in Ukraine against continued disruption to Black Sea exports. Profit-taking following a strong rally also contributed to the decline. Prices had reached $7.70 on August 28, marking a three-and-a-half-year high, before easing. Ukraine struck targets at the Russian port of Novorossiysk, including a naval base and an oil-loading terminal, renewing concerns over disruptions to Black Sea trade. President Volodymyr Zelenskiy said the attack targeted several facilities, while traders cited unconfirmed reports that grain infrastructure may also have been damaged. However, attention shifted back to diplomacy after the Kremlin said it hoped US-mediated talks would resume soon and identified Abu Dhabi as its preferred venue for negotiations. The prospect of renewed talks helped ease some of the market’s supply concerns.

### 2026-09-09 23:44:37  #其他

【多瑙河布達佩斯段水位降至0釐米】布達佩斯水務部門當地時間9月9日在社交媒體表示，當天，多瑙河布達佩斯段水位降至0釐米，但這並不意味著多瑙河中沒有水。布達佩斯水務部門表示，“0釐米”這一讀數，並不代表河流的深度。位於布達佩斯維加多廣場的水位標尺設有0釐米刻度，該數值對應的海拔是94.97米（以波羅的海海平面為基準）。布達佩斯水務部門指出，0釐米意味著多瑙河的水面恰好處於這一海拔高度。將零水位定在這一位置是因為其與多瑙河布達佩斯段歷史上的枯水位對齊——此基準設定於1943年，依據1834年枯水期記錄到的最低水位。因此，即便多瑙河水位顯示負值，也不意味多瑙河已乾涸。 (央視新聞)

US 10-Year Yield Rises to 3-Year High After Buyback — The yield on the 10-year US Treasury note rose to 4.85% on Wednesday, the highest since October of 2023, after the Treasury Department tripled the buyback on notes and bonds to take place tomorrow. The department will purchase $6 billion in off-the-run securities, compared with Secretary Bessent's earlier announcement that buybacks would at least double to $4 billion, among the multiple moves by Washington to supress long-maturity yields. Yields in the long-end of the curve have surged this year as high oil prices due to the war in Iran raised underlying inflation, risking a rate hike by the Federal Reserve in upcoming meetings. This was magnified by record-setting bond supply from corporate debt, with AI companies issuing north of $1.5 trillion in new issuance. On top of that, pressure on the Japanese yen drove Tokyo to repeatedly sell Treasury securities to defend the currency.

### 2026-09-09 22:59:42  #MKT News

【Loopholes in UK ban on imports from Israeli West Bank settlements】The UK will ban imports of goods produced in Israeli settlements in the occupied West Bank and create new powers to sanction individuals and firms that "support, assist or profit from" settlement activity. Practical enforcement gaps, however, limit near-term market impact. UK and Israel have a free trade agreement with bilateral trade of roughly £6 billion a year by 2025; official data show UK imports recorded from Palestine over the past four quarters totaled only about £6 million—equivalent to roughly 0.1% of projected UK‑Israel trade even if all were settlement goods. Origin identification is a key problem: settlement products are often incorporated into Israeli supply chains and exported as Israeli goods; one investigation found about one-sixth of Israeli fresh produce bound for Europe contains settlement-origin items. The UK requires importers seeking tariff preferences to declare code Y864 and give an origin postcode, but false declarations are difficult for customs to trace. The sanctions phraseology also leaves open questions for public contracting: an Al Jazeera review found roughly £2.1 billion of UK public-sector contracts awarded to 17 firms linked to settlements, with Motorola Solutions–related entities accounting for more than £1.7 billion, mostly via UK subsidiary Airwave Solutions. The government has not clarified whether designated firms would face contract termination or restructuring.

【首屆國際太空峰會在巴黎開幕 美航天企業取消行程】為期兩天的首屆國際太空峰會9日在法國巴黎開幕。多國領導人和政府官員、國際組織代表，以及來自航天機構和企業界人士參會，就太空事業快速發展背景下的相關科技、經濟與治理等議題展開討論。本次峰會由法國和德國共同主辦。據法國政府網站介紹，峰會目標是就太空事業領域所面臨的問題和挑戰促成國際對話。來自近120個國家和地區的代表團受邀與會。據官方日程，峰會主要聚焦三大議題：一是空間科技與探索的國際路徑；二是全球太空經濟；三是安全與治理，呼籲透過對話、防禦和夥伴關係確保空間安全。另據多家法國媒體援引美國《政治報》的報道說，由於美國政府施壓，太空探索技術公司和藍色起源公司等美航天企業取消峰會行程。峰會大使、法國宇航員托馬斯·佩斯凱就此對法國媒體說，太空事業受到了地緣政治干擾。（新華社）

### 2026-09-09 22:48:53  #Trading Economics #Economy #Poland #Interest Rate #Importance 1

National Bank of Poland Leaves Rates Unchanged as Expected — The National Bank of Poland kept its reference policy unchanged at 3.75% at its September 2026 meeting, in line with market expectations and marking the fifth consecutive meeting with rates held at their lowest level since 2022. Headline inflation accelerate to 3.4%, its highest level since June 2025, and bringing inflation just below the upper limit of the central bank's target of 2.5±1%, as tensions in the Middle East continue to lift energy prices. Fuel price caps also expired, although it could aid the government's budget. Meanwhile, the economy expanded at a faster-than-expected rate of 3.9% in the second quarter of 2026, as investment growth accelerated. Consumption slowed as wage growth decreased. Policymakers remained cautious, citing elevated uncertainty regarding the broader geopolitical situation and its impact on the economy. Future decisions will continue to take into account inflation and changes in economic and wage growth.

【俄官員：歐洲天然氣價格未來將再創新高】俄羅斯總統新聞秘書佩斯科夫9日表示，儘管歐洲天然氣價格近期快速上漲，但並未達到峰值，預計未來還將創下新高。 佩斯科夫在當天舉行的新聞釋出會上表示，即使以最快速度進行，歐洲也無法在冬季到來之前完成地下天然氣庫的補庫工作。他說，歐洲應該認識到有必要去尋找更便宜的能源來源，而俄羅斯的天然氣，包括管道天然氣和液化天然氣，都是最便宜的。 他還指出，“北溪”天然氣管道中仍有一條保持完好隨時可用，但是政治因素導致歐洲無法理性地思考問題，最終受損的卻是歐洲經濟和歐洲民眾。 據英國《金融時報》報道，歐洲天然氣儲存量目前僅為儲存能力的65.6%，為15年來同期最低水平。歐洲天然氣價格過去兩個月上漲超過75%，本週創下三年來新高。由於高氣價限制夏季補庫速度，而歐洲距離冬季能源需求高峰越來越近，能源供應和價格走勢的不確定性進一步增加。(新華社)

【摩根大通老將Hofmann加盟滙豐 助力拓展全球家族辦公室業務】滙豐控股聘請曾長期任職摩根大通的Hannes Hofmann，負責服務億萬富豪的私人投資機構。這是這家金融巨頭私人銀行業務進行的最新一輪管理層調整。滙豐在宣告中表示，Hofmann將於週四加入滙豐，出任全球家族辦公室負責人一職，常駐倫敦。他此前曾在花旗集團擔任類似職位，直至去年。與此同時，Cayman Wills也將於本月晚些時候從花旗加入滙豐，出任滙豐美國私人銀行主管。她此前曾協助管理花旗面向美國東北部超高淨值人士的服務業務。這些任命之前，花旗前高管Ida Liu已於今年初加入滙豐，負責這家總部位於倫敦的銀行的全球私人銀行業務，接替了Gabriel Castello。2024年底，在集團行政總裁艾橋智推動管理層調整之際，Annabel Spring離任，Castello隨後被任命為財富管理業務臨時執行長。

US Equities Pull Back — US stocks were lower on Wednesday as higher yields dented the outlook on corporate margins and consumer demand. The S&P 500 and the Dow fell around 0.5%, while fresh weakness for the tech sector pressured the Nasdaq 100 to a near 1% loss. Oil and fuel prices rose further as strikes between the US and Iran escalated, denting the likelihood of a resumption in Middle Eastern oil exports in the near future, and adding to inflationary concerns ahead of the Federal Reserve's meeting next week. Risks that higher borrowing costs would hamper equity markets seemingly rose after the resilience since the start of the war, in part due to higher rates on soaring debt by AI hyperscalers for datacenter development. Alphabet fell 3% after announcing a $15.1 billion buildout in Finland, while Amazon dropped 2% on another financing round in sterling. On the other hand, Meta jumped 4.5% after announcing a new AI agent.

### 2026-09-09 21:28:20  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy

【Russia's Arctic LNG-2 accelerates output in July-August as shadow tanker fleet expands】Russia's Arctic LNG-2, led by Novatek, ramped production in July and August to about 27 mln cubic meters of gas per day—almost double August 2025 levels—enabled by an expanded shadow tanker fleet; the capacity rise could translate into higher LNG output. Vessel-tracking compilations show the project now uses at least 20 ships, most with opaque ownership structures, up from 11 at end-December. Exports exceeded 500,000 tonnes in both July and August, record monthly highs, equivalent to roughly 700 mln cubic meters per month by Novatek's conversion. Arctic LNG-2 is central to Russia's plan to double LNG production; Western sanctions after Russia's full-scale invasion of Ukraine pushed the original timetable past 2030.

【智庫警示：AI 資料中心在英國創造的就業崗位，將不及科技行業預估水平】智庫 Verdant 週三表示，英國政府與行業領軍企業採信了科技行業協會 techUK 廣為引用的資料，批准新建一批資料中心。但 Verdant 指出，該資料誇大了資料中心實際能夠創造的新增長期固定崗位規模。主張收緊資料中心開發管控的 Verdant 估算，英國現有資料中心直接吸納就業約 4400 人；而 techUK 的估算數字為 24300 人。對於規劃中的在建專案，Verdant 預測到 2035 年將產生約 10400 個長期運營崗位，大約僅為 techUK 預測 40200 個崗位的四分之一。該智庫在研讀英國 20 個規劃中資料中心的規劃檔案與公開人員資料後測算，按容量加權平均值，每兆瓦僅能創造約 1.2 個運營崗位。

### 2026-09-09 21:26:39  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #DAX #Impact bearish #EURUSD

【German power prices hit highest since June as wind plunge boosts fossil‑fuel generation demand】German power prices rose to their highest since June as an expected sharp drop in wind output increased reliance on pricier fossil‑fuel generation. Epex Spot data showed Thursday day‑ahead prices at €191.82/MWh; the 7pm contract spiked to €460.86/MWh, the highest for that hour so far in 2024. Models forecast wind generation around 3.5 GW at 8pm — under one‑third of the year‑to‑date average — lifting demand for gas‑fired output ahead of the heating season. The move coincides with Middle East tensions that have pushed wholesale gas prices higher. Germany’s large weather‑dependent renewables fleet makes it particularly exposed: when wind and PV fall, the country shifts to more expensive fossil generation and increases imports.

【BofA: Oracle stock has priced in downside but not the potential for revenue reacceleration】BofA warns Oracle reports FY2027 Q1 on Sept. 10 and its stock is down ~25% since the prior quarter, reflecting concerns about data‑center profitability, rising component costs and wider credit spreads. BofA says investors should focus on three items: progress on data‑center capacity builds and the speed at which RPO (signed but unrecognized revenue) converts to revenue; capital expenditure and financing needs; and core software trends. BofA judges the market has largely priced balance‑sheet stress but not the upside if data‑center builds reach a tipping point and revenue growth reaccelerates, making the pre‑earnings risk/reward attractive. BofA maintains a $240 PT based on 2027E P/E of 26.5x; key risks are data‑center build delays, weaker enterprise software spending, adverse FX and AI lab activity that could erode long‑term software value (BofA report, Sept. 8).

【挪威為已故國王哈拉爾五世舉行葬禮】挪威9日在首都奧斯陸為已故國王哈拉爾五世舉行葬禮。挪威王室成員、多國王室成員及外國政要等出席葬禮。當天中午12時，哈拉爾五世的靈柩從王宮啟程，在儀仗隊護送下經過奧斯陸市中心前往奧斯陸大教堂。數千名民眾沿途送別。挪威國王哈康八世等王室成員參加送葬隊伍。葬禮開始時，挪威全國默哀一分鐘。挪威議會議長馬蘇德·加拉哈尼和首相斯特勒在葬禮上分別致悼詞。根據計劃，葬禮結束後，哈拉爾五世的靈柩將由儀仗隊護送至阿克斯胡斯城堡教堂，安葬於教堂內的王室墓穴。哈拉爾五世生於1937年2月，1991年1月在其父奧拉夫五世去世後繼承王位。今年8月28日，哈拉爾五世在奧斯陸去世，享年89歲。（新華社）

### 2026-09-09 21:17:01  #公司 #焦點

【誰擠進了蘋果摺疊屏手機供應鏈？8家公司回應】蘋果首款摺疊屏iPhone即將登場，其背後的供應鏈也逐漸浮出水面。藍思科技（300433.SZ）證券部工作人員告訴記者，公司關於蘋果摺疊屏相關訂單的情況可以參考公司半年報以及近期投資者交流內容。

宜安科技（300328.SZ）則主要涉及摺疊屏手機鉸鏈主軸。公司工作人員告訴記者，公司生產的是鉸鏈中的主軸零部件，產品交付給鉸鏈組裝廠。“我們只做幾十個鉸鏈零部件中的一個，鉸鏈組裝廠有很多終端客戶，最終客戶的情況我們也不清楚。”

精研科技（300709.SZ）此前已經佈局摺疊屏相關MIM精密零部件和鉸鏈組裝業務。對於是否參與蘋果摺疊屏手機供應鏈，公司工作人員表示，具體合作情況受到保密協議限制，不便透露。

東睦股份（600114.SH）也被市場認為可能參與蘋果摺疊屏手機MIM零部件供應。公司證券部工作人員對記者表示，具體情況目前並不清楚，銷售端的資訊無法直接獲得。

領益智造（002600.SZ）同樣未直接披露客戶名稱。公司工作人員表示，由於與客戶簽署了保密協議，具體客戶和訂單情況不便公開。

長盈精密（300115.SZ）則被市場關注於鈦合金中框等精密結構件。記者就相關情況致電公司，公司工作人員表示，公司消費電子業務與國內外頭部客戶均有合作，但由於客戶對供應商管理嚴格，具體客戶資訊不便披露。

福蓉科技（603327.SH）的回應則更加明確。針對“是否為蘋果摺疊屏手機提供鋁製結構件”的問題，公司工作人員表示：“我們沒有給蘋果做摺疊屏（結構件），主要做它的平板和筆記本（結構件）。”

鵬鼎控股（002938.SZ）則涉及柔性電路板。對於是否為蘋果摺疊屏手機提供高彎折FPC，公司工作人員表示，公司與大客戶在全產品線上均有合作，具體供應哪些產品要視情況而定，訂單情況不便透露，可以參考公司月度營收情況。（第一財經）

【中方釋出2026 APEC“中國年”能源領域東道主自主貢獻《提升電力系統韌性的推薦解決方案》】9月7日，APEC電力系統韌性建設研討會在北京舉辦。中方釋出2026 APEC“中國年”能源領域東道主自主貢獻《提升電力系統韌性的推薦解決方案》。 該方案聚焦電力系統安全穩定執行核心需求，吸納中國電力系統防災減災、應急保障與靈活調節實踐經驗，以強化裝備與設施支撐夯實物理基礎、以完善市場與政策體系最佳化制度環境、以深化治理與應急協同築牢安全防線為核心框架，覆蓋源—網—荷—儲全環節基礎設施與技術裝備建設，形成可複製推廣、普遍適配的電力系統韌性提升路徑，為各經濟體結合自身國情推進韌性建設提供參考。 該方案在中國國家能源局指導下，由中方參與APEC能源合作伙伴網路成員單位國家電網公司牽頭編制，依託APEC能源自籌基金專案落地實施，是2026 APEC“中國年”能源領域5項東道主自主貢獻之一。來自APEC各經濟能源部門、有關國際組織、能源企業、研究機構等百餘名代表參加會議。（國家能源局）

【伊朗譴責美軍襲擊伊朗船隻稱已回以打擊】伊朗外交部9日發表宣告，譴責美軍8日對數艘伊朗船隻發動襲擊。作為回應，伊朗武裝部隊已對美國軍事基地和船隻實施打擊。 宣告稱，美軍在波斯灣和阿曼灣對伊朗船隻發動襲擊，是美方對伊軍事行動、海上封鎖和經濟戰的一部分，違反《聯合國憲章》和國際法，加劇地區緊張局勢，威脅地區和國際和平與安全。 宣告稱，針對美方襲擊伊朗船隻，伊朗武裝部隊已對美國在地區內的軍事基地和設施實施打擊，並對數艘美方船隻實施“防禦性打擊”。宣告還稱，伊朗武裝部隊在行使合法自衛權時“不會有任何猶豫”，將以適當方式對敵人的一切軍事侵略作出堅決回應。 伊朗外交部同時敦促聯合國安理會和聯合國秘書長立即採取行動，制止美方公然破壞國際和平與安全的對伊軍事行動。 美軍中央司令部8日在社交媒體釋出訊息說，在伊朗對一艘美軍艦發動攻擊後，美軍當天摧毀了5艘伊朗油輪。伊朗伊斯蘭革命衛隊9日發表宣告稱，作為對美軍在波斯灣打擊伊朗油輪的回應，革命衛隊當天對兩艘美國軍艦和8艘油輪實施打擊，還對10艘違規船隻實施了打擊。美軍中央司令部隨後在社交媒體上發文否認其海軍艦艇被伊朗襲擊。(新華社)

### 2026-09-09 20:53:01  #公司 #市場 #國際

【世界黃金協會：歐洲8月黃金ETF流入79億美元，創區域歷史新高】世界黃金協會表示，歐洲黃金ETF 8月持續貢獻全球主要資金流入，當月買入規模達79億美元，創下區域歷史最高月度紀錄。除支撐北美黃金需求的諸多因素外，歐洲投資者還持續擔憂財政可持續性以及主權融資成本高企。在此背景下，黃金作為投資組合分散工具、主權債務替代資產的價值，仍是重要的需求推手。繼7月金價反彈後買盤持續走強，也說明投資者越來越認為夏季金價回撥是重建戰略倉位的機會，而非減持視窗。英國（44億美元）仍是歐洲資金流入主力，創下本國史上第二大月度淨流入。法國當月資金流入15億美元，創下歷史新高，進一步體現歐洲各地投資者的廣泛配置需求。

### 2026-09-09 20:52:27  #其他

【廣東省省委書記到深圳調研並主持召開座談會，視察大普微電子、江波龍電子、德明利和佰維儲存等高科技企業】9月8日至9日，省委書記黃坤明到深圳市，深入高科技企業調研並主持召開座談會，研究進一步做好深圳工作，強調深圳要認真學習貫徹習近平總書記對廣東、對深圳系列重要講話和重要指示精神，始終牢記囑託、感恩奮進，保持永爭第一、不為人後的幹勁闖勁，以走在前列、勇當尖兵的責任擔當紮實推動高質量發展、現代化建設，奮力把新時代經濟特區辦得更好、辦得水平更高。
半導體與積體電路是省市重點發展的戰略性新興產業，近年來深圳已培育招引了一批初創企業、優質骨幹企業和龍頭專案，呈現出集聚發展的良好態勢。黃坤明先後來到大普微電子、江波龍電子、德明利和佰維儲存等高科技企業，聽取企業負責人關於發展歷程、產業生態、行業前景的介紹，察看有關核心產品，並走進研發實驗室，詳細瞭解關鍵裝置、材料和製備工藝。黃坤明指出，隨著人工智慧技術的快速進步和規模化應用，算力需求迎來爆發式增長，為半導體與積體電路產業帶來前所未有的發展機遇，希望企業以時不我待的緊迫感搶佔風口，加強與上下游夥伴的協同攻關，繼續在技術創新與產品迭代上下功夫，加快成果轉化、工藝升級和產能提升，不斷強化技術積累、擴大領先優勢，持續增厚築牢產品技術“護城河”，牢牢佔據市場主動。黃坤明肯定企業始終堅守創業初心，堅定投入、持續投入，歷經千辛萬苦，以多年磨一劍的毅力，實現跨越式發展，這啟示我們搶佔新賽道產業，要樹立長遠眼光、戰略思維，對於選準看定的新興產業、未來產業要抱定長期主義，保持戰略耐心，堅定不移大規模投入、長週期支撐，幫助企業克服困難、經受考驗，完成產品技術躍遷和市場驗證前的關鍵跨越，共同迎來產業發展的曙光和春天。黃坤明強調，繼續做大做強半導體與積體電路產業，深圳有基礎、有積澱，要下定決心加大培育扶持力度，充分發揮產品更新快、產業協同配套能力強等優勢，加快推進重大專案建設和產能爬坡，持續做優晶片設計，補齊晶圓製造短板弱項，拓展豐富終端產品和應用場景，實現設計、製造、封測全鏈條全棧自主可控，緊密對接企業產能擴張、技術升級、最佳化佈局需求，致力完善人才引育、投融資支援、用地用能等服務保障，推動產業規模擴大、能級提升，有力支撐我省打造積體電路產業“第三極”。

【國金證券牟一凌：配置首推能源鏈條，工程機械、電網裝置等出口鏈條具備中期配置價值】9月9日，國金證券首席策略官牟一凌在國金證券2026秋季策略會上表示，AI融資擴張支撐了美國增長與資本回流，但投資回報的不確定性正在上升。他指出，美國高實際利率壓制了非美需求，但新興市場的基建和資源開發需求更多是被高融資成本抑制，並未消失；若AI投資放緩帶動美國實際利率下降、美元走弱，資金重新流向非美市場，實物資產重估的新路徑有望開啟，黃金等實物資產的儲備價值也可能再次受到重視。配置上，牟一凌指出，當前全球原油庫存下降、歐美煉廠開工率接近滿負荷，低庫存使能源價格對供應擾動更為敏感，建議優先關注煤炭、石油、油運及煉化等能化鏈條；銅、金、鋁等有色資產有望受益於美元與實際利率壓制緩和；高股息、低波動的紅利資產仍是絕對收益資金的重要配置方向；工程機械、電網裝置等出口鏈條具備中期配置價值。

### 2026-09-09 20:18:48  #Trading Economics #Economy #Mexico #Inflation Rate #Importance 2

Mexico Inflation Rate Accelerates as Expected — Mexico's annual inflation rate quickened for the first time in five months to 3.26% in August 2026 from 3.12% in the previous month. The reading came broadly in line with market expectations, although moving away from Banxico's 3% target. Inflation accelerated sharply for food products, rising to 1.49% from 0.78% in July, and transport, which edged up to 3.43% after a 3.21% rise. Additional upward pressure was due to higher inflation for clothing and footwear (2.16% vs 1.6%), recreation and culture (3.85% vs 3.63%) and education (6.02% vs 5.91%). Energy inflation, however, slowed sharply to 0.81% from 1.16%, while inflation also softened for housing and utilities (3.06% vs 3.23%). Core inflation also eased, slowing to 3.88% from 3.95%. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.2%, accelerating from a 0.03% rise in the prior month.

【瑞銀：美股對債券收益率上升最為敏感 AI板塊高估值風險突出】瑞銀警告稱，如果債券收益率持續上升並開始壓制股市，美國股市可能是發達市場中最脆弱的市場之一，尤其是人工智慧產業鏈相關股票。瑞銀HOLT分析師Michel Lerner表示，中東危機、通脹、財政擔憂以及AI供應鏈龐大的債務融資需求，已將政府債券收益率推升至全球金融危機前以來的高位，而美股估值仍處於較高水平。瑞銀指出，許多AI價值鏈公司當前股價隱含了創紀錄的未來現金流預期，因此對資本成本上升尤為敏感，這也使美國成為發達經濟體中“久期最長”的股票市場。與此同時，更高的利率將抬升增長專案的回報門檻，對已面臨投資回報質疑的AI基礎設施支出尤其不利。瑞銀建議，在高利率環境下，投資者應優先關注具有較強競爭壁壘、資產負債表穩健且久期風險較低的公司，並對估值較高、資本支出超過內部現金創造能力的企業保持謹慎。

US Futures Extend Pullback — US equity futures were lower for a third session on Wednesday as higher yields dented the outlook on corporate margins and consumer demand. Contracts for the S&P 500 and the Dow fell around 0.5%, while fresh weakness for the tech sector pressured the Nasdaq 100 to a near 1% loss. Oil and fuel prices rose further as strikes between the US and Iran escalated, denting the likelihood of a resumption in Middle Eastern oil exports in the near future, and adding to inflationary concerns ahead of the Federal Reserve's meeting next week. Risks that higher borrowing costs would hamper equity markets seemingly rose after the resilience since the start of the war, in part due to higher rates on soaring debt by AI hyperscalers for datacenter development. Alphabet fell 2.3% after announcing a $15.1 billion buildout in Finland, while Amazon dropped 1.5% on another financing round in sterling. On the other hand, Meta soared 5% after announcing a new AI agent.

US 10-Year Yield Rises to 3-Year High — The yield on the 10-year US Treasury note rose past 4.8% on Wednesday, the highest since October of 2023, as higher energy prices supported the case for interest rate hikes by the Federal Reserve this year. The US reportedly struck multiple Iranian tankers and Iranian forces continued to attack US forces in the Middle East, escalating the ongoing war and prolonging the likely suspension of fuel from the region. Higher fuel prices had already impacted gauges of underlying inflation. This drove multiple FOMC members to call for higher interest rates to combat the threat of price growth, including Chairman Warsh, who noted that a rate hike is warranted if inflation increases further. Meanwhile, estimates that AI companies raised $1.5 trillion in debt this year limited primary dealers' allocation for government securities, also lifting yields. Treasuries were pressured across the curve despite Secretary Bessent's warning against shorting bonds ahead of his buyback announcement.

Sensex Closes at 3-Month Low — India's BSE Sensex extended early losses to close about 1.1% down at 74,764 on Wednesday, the lowest since June, marking the third session of decreases. The escalation of tensions between the US and Iran in the Persian Gulf region added to concerns about global energy supplies, pushing oil prices higher and fanning inflation concerns. Continued foreign fund outflows also weighed on investor sentiment. Selling pressure was seen across most sectors, led by IT, financials and FMGC stocks. HCLTech, Infosys, Tech Mahindra and TCS shed between 2% and 4.6%. Other laggards included HDFC Bank (-2.2%), ICICI Bank (-0.7%) and Reliance Industries (-1%). On the upside, Adani Ports outperformed, climbing 3.7%, after winning the bid to develop and operate two dry bulk berths at Paradip Port in Odisha. The project will lift domestic cargo capacity to 671 MMT, bringing it closer to its 1 billion-tonne 2030 target. Tata Steel (2.4%), Trent (1.1%) and NTPC (0.5%) also recorded gains.

### 2026-09-09 19:13:55  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Mortgage Applications #Importance 1

US Mortgage Applications Fall Most in 1 Month — The volume of mortgage applications in the US fell by 2.7% in the first week of September, the sharpest decline in a little over one month, according to data compiled by the Mortgage Bankers Association. The result was aligned with the sharp increase in mortgage rates in the period, with that on the benchmark 30-year fixed rate averaging a 15-month high of 6.58%. Yields on longer maturity Treasury securities soared at the turn of the month as high energy prices, elevated debt supply, and widening deficits limited exposure to duration in fixed income. Consequently, 8.5% of all applications were adjustable-rate mortgages, the largest ratio since June last year. Applications to refinance a mortgage, which are more sensitive to short-term changes in interest rates, fell by 6%. In turn, applications for a mortgage to buy a home were nearly flat.

### 2026-09-09 19:06:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Mortgage Rate #Importance 2

US Mortgage Rates Hit 15-Month High — The average contract rate for a 30-year fixed mortgage in the US rose to 6.85% in the week ended September 4, its highest level since June 2025, according to the Mortgage Bankers Association. The increase followed a rise in Treasury yields as renewed Middle East tensions fueled inflation concerns, while stronger US labor-market data reinforced expectations for a September rate hike. Mortgage rates have risen nearly 80 basis points since the US and Israel began strikes against Iran in late February. Meanwhile, total mortgage applications fell 2.7%, reversing a 0.8% increase the previous week. Refinancing applications dropped 6.2%, while purchase applications edged down 0.2%.

### 2026-09-09 19:03:35  #其他

【中國海警圓滿完成2026年北太平洋公海漁業執法巡航任務】9月9日上午，由中國海警長山艦和五虎艦組成的艦艇編隊圓滿完成2026年北太平洋公海漁業執法巡航任務，順利返港。這是中國海警在北太平洋公海開展的第11次巡航。此次巡航歷時45天，航程19800餘海里，航時2100餘小時。巡航期間，中國海警艦艇編隊根據聯合國大會相關決議、《北太平洋公海漁業資源養護和管理公約》等規定，加大公海漁業法律法規和養護管理措施宣傳，加強作業漁船巡查監管，做好區域漁業管理註冊漁船資訊核對。共觀察記錄各國漁船95艘，登臨檢查20艘，直升機公海飛行執法7架次，有效履行了公海執法職責，維護了北太平洋漁業生產秩序。作為海洋漁業大國和《北太平洋公海漁業資源養護和管理公約》成員國，中國海警派艦艇赴北太平洋開展漁業執法巡航，對中國參與全球海洋治理、保護公海漁業資源具有重要意義。下步，中國海警將持續深入開展公海漁業執法巡航，積極履行公約義務，為有效維護公海漁業生產秩序發揮應有作用。

### 2026-09-09 18:51:08  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Cocoa Futures at 2-Week Low — Cocoa futures traded below $5,900 per tonne, the lowest in two weeks, amid signs of ample supply. ICE-monitored inventories have risen to 3.44 million bags, the highest level in two years, underscoring the improvement in near-term availability. The prospect of a larger crop in top producer Ivory Coast also weighed on prices. Industry assessments point to a similar improvement in supply conditions. Barry Callebaut AG, the world’s largest cocoa processor, said the global cocoa market is well supplied and better positioned to manage supply disruptions than it was during the 2023/24 El Niño event, which drove prices to record highs. Still, the outlook for the coming season remains uncertain, with weather posing a key risk to production. Adverse conditions could undermine crop prospects and temper expectations for a sustained improvement in global supply.
